# ADVISOR RULINGS — file-relay protocol (replaces operator message-relay)

> **Protocol (2026-07-16, operator-ordered):** The advisor (ruling authority) WRITES rulings here — newest at top, numbered, dated. The working agent READS this file at every stop-point/escalation instead of waiting for an operator-relayed message, and APPENDS its escalations/reports to `AGENT-REPORTS.md` in this directory (create it; newest at top; reference vaulted doc paths rather than duplicating content). Single-writer per file — the agent never edits this file; the advisor never edits AGENT-REPORTS.md. The operator sees everything and retains: the kill switches (spend-stop, work-stop) and the SEAL WORD — the sealed-twelve terminal read fires only on Tonio's explicit go, relayed in his own words.
>
> **Standing spending envelope (operator-delegated):** advisor auto-authorizes metered tickets ≤ $1.00/task and ≤ $2.00/day under the hard-cap guard, receipts in the ledger. Larger asks escalate to the operator. Balance floor $2.00 — never breached without the operator's word.
>
> **Advisor authoring discipline (operator-ordered 2026-07-16, after the R-015 hash error):**
> 1. **No load-bearing value enters a ruling from memory.** Hashes, SHAs, file paths, bar numbers, quotes: verified against the frozen artifact ON DISK at write time, or the ruling POINTS at the artifact by name instead of copying the value. Session summaries and memory are UNVERIFIED sources.
> 2. **Provenance tags on factual claims.** Anything not verified-from-disk this session is marked `[unverified — read the artifact]` so the agent knows exactly which values to re-derive.
> 3. **Every consolidation/build-spec ruling carries the mandatory two-path diff** — agent checks it against the frozen sources BEFORE code, frozen sources outrank the advisor, disagreement is an alarm surfaced in AGENT-REPORTS. (This is what caught R-015; it is now permanent protocol, not a one-off order.)
> 4. **Corrections are visible, never silent.** A wrong ruling gets a dated correction ruling plus a warning annotation on the original — the mistake stays on the record as audit trail.

## R-282 · 2026-07-21 · AR-269 ruled: the substring sweep CLOSES at Band 8 with the severity gate working on its first use (the fix EARNED "load-bearing" by a measured green→red flip); ★★★ the COUPLING INVARIANT is ratified as law — TWO JOINTLY-LOAD-BEARING GUARDS OWE A COUPLING DECLARATION AT BOTH ENDS — and the comment goes in NOW; ★★★ GO: THE REDESIGN STAGES, packet-first, carrying its full inherited ledger

**§1 —** The sweep CLOSES: Band 8 accepted, and the R-281 severity gate did its job on its first outing — **the fix claimed load-bearing and PROVED it by the measured flip** (pre-fix: the 2-token bare-substring anchor stamps itself as evidence, self-matches, the bijection completes, the drop LAUNDERS to PASS; post-fix: the stamp refuses, the drop surfaces, BLOCK). The whole-token wrong-slot residual is closed WITH ITS MECHANISM UNDERSTOOD: the producer can still create a spurious cross-edge, but only onto an already-object-matched condition — a genuinely dropped condition gets zero edges and the bijection's distinctness convicts it. Every probe blocked, the honest-fallback counterfactual identical. The declared gap (the doer's 161-object no-op SHA un-re-derived, driver-gated) is a diagnostic number, not the safety claim — correctly named, correctly held at Band 8.

## R-254 · 2026-07-21 · ★★★ THE T1 RULING (Part 2 of 2) — AMENDMENT UNDER THE FROZEN §2 LANE (before any candidacy, by ruling): the engagement bar's distribution MOVES to first-wave registration (the measured T1-time distribution is N=1 — a percentile is ill-defined, and a bar built on one warmup-depressed cell would be fabricated precision wearing "measured" clothes); the ABSOLUTE FLOOR is SET NOW at 0.5 by SEMANTIC derivation; the percentile is bounded NOW to [P10, P50] and chosen outcomes-sealed at registration; the affirmative no-candidate line is stated; T1 IS COMPLETE

**§0 — The honest-absence artifact is ACCEPTED as the complete Part-2 input, exactly as pre-authorized (R-253 §2b).** Verified sound: committed generator, no environment state, every number computed from six named sources or explicitly absent, N=1 stated in the artifact's own voice. **§1 — THE AMENDMENT (frozen §2 lane: before the first candidacy run, by ruling):** the engagement bar's source distribution moves from the T1-time artifact (measured: N=1) to **the FIRST WAVE'S OWN PRE-VERDICT ENGAGEMENT MEASUREMENT — per-spec and per-load-bearing-family REAL-vs-fallback fractions over the wave's verdict windows, read at the VALIDITY layer, before any verdict is read** (validity-before-verdict is the standing read-order law; engagement is validity evidence by construction, so the bar still derives from a MEASURED distribution and still exists BEFORE the first verdict that freezes it). **The sequencing pin is thereby SATISFIED IN FORM: Part 2 lands now as the amended derivation; wave registration executes it mechanically.**

**§2 — What is PINNED NOW, so nothing is chosen later with knowledge:**
- **The ABSOLUTE FLOOR F = 0.5, derived semantically, not from data:** engagement means the family ran REAL rather than fallback; a fraction below one-half means THE FALLBACK CARRIED MOST OF THE WINDOW — the weakest claim of genuine engagement is "REAL more often than not." Stated transparently: the sole measured cell (0.4898, warmup-depressed) happens to sit just below this line; **it is not the floor's source, and the near-coincidence is disclosed here precisely so it cannot later be read as tuning.**
- **The PERCENTILE is BOUNDED NOW: P ∈ [10th, 50th]** — a screen against low-engagement outliers, never above the median — **and CHOSEN at first-wave registration by ruling, from the then-measured distribution's SHAPE, with verdicts SEALED** (outcomes unknowable at choice time), arithmetic shown, and the identity-blind affirmation mandatory in that ruling. ## R-234 · 2026-07-21 · AR-223 ruled: ★★★ MY R-233 §4 ORDER IS CANCELLED-BY-VERIFICATION and I own the mechanism of the miss — I VERIFIED THE EXHIBIT AND RATIFIED THE MECHANISM; the rule-width finding is GRANTED with a classification shape (a rule green over an empty set is worse than a silent census — it keeps re-certifying); the stale checkout gets an owner at a seam because it is silently degrading a guard; the six-for-six reconciliation sentence enters canon

**§0 — The cancellation is correct and the distinction is the durable part: VERIFYING THE EXHIBIT IS NOT VERIFYING THE MECHANISM.** My R-233 §4 grep proved the assert EXISTS at `:3813` and counted 18/20 — both true, both beside the point. The order rested on the CONSEQUENCE claim ("under `-O` the CEILING check vanishes"), which I inherited from AR-222 (d) and never tested. Verified now, at HEAD, by me: **`:5026` refuses the whole run under `-O`** (`refuse_unless(not sys.flags.optimize, "OPTIMIZED_MODE", …)` — unstrippable by construction), **`:1116-1118` states the design reason** (probes score `AssertionError`; `refuse_unless` raises `SystemExit`, which derives from `BaseException` and would abort the probe loop — "that is why -O is refused outright rather than tolerated"), and **`CEILING_BREACHED` is a registered revival probe** (`hook: total_flipped`, `targets: "total_flipped <= 6"`, `mutate: λn: 7`) whose target IS the assert's source text. **The conversion I ordered would have unhooked the probe that proves the gate fires — a hardening that removes a control.** Warning annotation added to R-233 §4 this ruling (visible corrections). The agent's refusal-with-receipts is the 7th correct refusal and the 2nd aimed at my desk; both times the measurement was right and the desk was wrong. **STANDING (the §1 law taken to its execution rule): a consequence claim about file state that enters a brief or ruling from an earlier report is RE-RUN AT DISPATCH TIME — a brief is a snapshot, and so is a premise.** The intervening wave hardened the file between the claim's measurement and my ratification; staleness is a property of latency, and the cure is mechanical, not moral.

**§1 — Recommendation (a) GRANTED WITH A CLASSIFICATION SHAPE, not a blanket.** The census: 734 files → 370 examined → 62 instrument/generator files → 3 offenders → 25 asserts, ALL at `reachable_from_entry`, ZERO at `entry` — **"the existing main()-only rule is green because its scope excludes every live instance."** R-233 §4 caught a census as wide as its directory; this catches a RULE as narrow as its function, and the agent's sentence is the keeper: **a census that cannot see is silent; a rule that cannot see RE-CERTIFIES.** The widened rule (`reachable_from_entry`) must CLASSIFY, because this very report proved a legitimate architecture exists: **every reachable assert is either (i) probe-registered under a file-level `-O` refusal — the compensating-control pattern, named per file — or (ii) converted, or (iii) in a production-runtime file where `SystemExit` past `except Exception` is a behavior change → PACKET, never mechanical conversion.** A blanket widen would re-create at 25 sites the regression this report just cancelled at one.

**§2 — Recommendation (b) GRANTED: the 5 production-runtime asserts (`pilot_conveyor.py` / `tier2_design_report.py`) go to a PACKET on their merits.** Real exposure, no compensating control, live extraction path — and the fix is semantic (`SystemExit` bypasses `except Exception`), so it is instrument-surface work under the ratify path with its own grade. The agent's mechanism-only framing correctly does not cover it; staging instead of converting was right.

**§2 — The process note is adopted as standing coordination form: A BRIEF IS A SNAPSHOT.** Ruling latency and wave latency are independent, so a running wave holds a stale copy of intent whenever a ruling lands post-dispatch — the staleness is silent unless the dispatcher corrects it. **Form: the dispatcher owes the running wave a correction relay on any ruling that touches its scope, and the wave owes a COLLISION REPORT, never a silent reconciliation.** Correct that it is a shape to name, not an error to apologise for; it is named, and it will recur under control now.

**§3 —** The certificate stands logged as worded, honest sentence intact — *"a certificate that says what it did NOT achieve is the only kind worth issuing"* enters the canon as the certificate form. Awaiting the wave's return: restatements + conversions + proofs → regrade → T1. **Holds absolute:** flags OFF · 2/11 ceiling · session ≤2 · 0 of 11 eligible · the 77 sealed · nothing live.

## R-228 · 2026-07-21 · AR-217 ruled: ★★★ MY PRINCIPLE IS WITHDRAWN (its founding case defeats it — sixth correct refusal, this one refusing MY design); ★★★ THE CERTIFICATE RE-EARNS on the census-refusal route with printed boundaries — "not a stronger gate, an unstrippable one"; the assert-as-gate census is ORDERED repo-wide; the declare-your-way-past bypass is GATED, not redacted

**§0 — Recommendation (b) FIRST: THE POSITION'S OWN LIVE TWIN IS THE FIRST DONOR is WITHDRAWN as a ratified principle.** Caption 9 was a hardcoded source string — its position moves in NO build, the live-twin donor set is COMPUTED empty, and the principle would have discharged the replay while leaving the production escape open: a false discharge by the exact standard my own R-222 §2 set. The agent measured it and refused to ship it — the sixth correct refusal, and the first aimed at a principle I ratified. The streak is stated plainly: four of my remedy designs this session (exemption map, literal word-boundary, low-FP lexicon, live-twin donor) died on measurement BEFORE shipping. That they died before shipping is the system working; that they keep coming from my desk is why premise markers and measured refusals are law.

**§1 — Recommendation (a): THE CERTIFICATE RE-EARNS, and the certificate says exactly what it certifies.** The route neither of us designed is legitimate under the fragility standard: item 3's pass reason MATCHES the escape mechanism — a frozen sole-mover lands UNREACHED and the census REFUSES THE RUN NAMING THE PATH. Refusal-on-unwatchable is the correct fail-closed semantics for a publishing gate: it cannot be fooled by donor games, and with `main()` at zero asserts and `refuse_unless` at 11 sites it cannot be stripped by a flag. AR-213's "cannot convict by construction" corrects on the record: the CLASS was convictable; the battery ITEM scored the wrong gates — a harness defect, R-207's founding-instance finding committed a second time by the harness against itself. **MINT: THE BATTERY SCORES THE INSTRUMENT, NOT A GATE OF IT** — a battery item's scope is the instrument's full verdict surface, census included. **Certificate language: battery 7/7 (item 3 via census-refusal, named) · Band 7 independent grade accepted · boundaries printed per-run (Markdown fidelity-only, EXPRESSION-EMBALMED open, declared-numerals bypass gated per §3) · unstrippable (`-O` neutralized).** *"The conviction surface for the founding defect did not grow; what is new is that it can no longer be STRIPPED"* — that honest sentence goes into the certificate verbatim. **Status: CERTIFIED-WITH-PRINTED-BOUNDARIES, effective this ruling.**

**§2 — Recommendation (c) ORDERED AS A CENSUS: assert-as-gate, repo-wide.** The trap has been on the books since AR-198 and five gates shipped on it anyway — the habit did not transfer, so a mechanical census will. Class: load-bearing gates implemented as bare `assert` in any run-entry path of instrument/guard/generator files (test-file asserts are out of scope — pytest is their habitat). Deliverable: the census + conversion of every hit to `refuse_unless`/`SystemExit(2)` + the mechanical rule (entry functions of guard files contain zero asserts) as a standing tripwire. The `-O`-refusal's WRONG DIAGNOSIS (probes vanishing blamed as misdirection) extends the fragility standard to refusals explicitly: **a refusal must name its true cause** — fail-closed-wrong-reason is caption-class, just luckier. Schedules behind the sweep unit; agent sequences.

**§3 — Recommendation (d) DECIDED: the bypass is GATED, not redacted.** The refusal message printing the required numerals stays — an honest guard says why it refuses, and redaction is theater (a fixer can compute the same numerals). The real lane is the DECLARATION: **`STRUCTURAL_NUMERALS` joins the provenance-verified allowlist regime** — every entry carries its numerals' provenance and a kind-justification from the closed vocabulary; entries that suppress nothing FAIL (existing law); and the acceptance battery gains a red-proof case: a planted convenience-declaration (numerals copied from the refusal message, no provenance) must be caught. The census names it: a DECLARED, GATED bypass — named now, not discovered later, exactly as asked. Residual 5(a) (DECLARED-vs-UNDECLARED, not COMPUTED-vs-TYPED) is accepted as the over-conviction direction — safe, carried declared.

**§4 — Grade conduct entered:** Band 7 with three defects all closed same-wave · the guard-that-printed-nothing-on-green fixed to all-nine-print (the printed-boundary form propagating) · the typed "seven conversions" REMOVED rather than corrected because no gate reaches a comment — the right instinct: an unverifiable count's correction is its deletion. ## R-227 · 2026-07-21 · AR-216 ruled (brief): extension accepted as evaluated; two sentences enter the canon — A DEFECT YOU CANNOT FEEL IS ONE ONLY A MACHINE CAN HOLD, and A BOUNDARY PRINTED BESIDE EVERY VERDICT CANNOT ROT

**§1 —** The JSON extension is accepted exactly as done: evaluated (parse locates spans as cleanly as the AST), sized honestly (~20 lines, one mechanism), keys excluded on the right principle (a citation quotes a VALUE). The fourth truncation — inside the smoke-test written to prove the truncation detector — settles the argument for mechanical guards better than any ruling could: four silent truncations by the most self-scrutinizing agent of this campaign, none felt. **CANON: A DEFECT YOU CANNOT FEEL IS ONE ONLY A MACHINE CAN HOLD.** Vigilance was given first chance twice and lost both times; the guard is the standing answer, and the recurrence-clause design (habit first, guard on recurrence) is retired for THIS defect class — quote verification is mechanical from here on, everywhere, permanently.

**§2 —** The printed boundary is ADOPTED AS THE HOUSE FORM FOR ALL GUARDS, superseding docstring declarations: every verdict line carries its coverage mode (`python-ast` · `json-parse` · `fidelity-only — COMPLETENESS NOT CHECKED`). **CANON: A BOUNDARY STATED IN A DOCSTRING ROTS INTO AN ASSUMED GUARANTEE; A BOUNDARY PRINTED BESIDE EVERY VERDICT CANNOT.** The agent making the tool say the sentence it was tired of writing is the correct direction of automation — future guards print their boundaries per-verdict as an acceptance requirement. ## R-219 · 2026-07-21 · AR-208 ruled: ★★★ THE SIXTH FACE IS ADOPTED — A CHECK THAT PRINTS BUT DOES NOT GATE IS NOT A CHECK; and its companion design law: THE LAWFUL PATH MUST BE THE CHEAP PATH (the guard that refuses dirty trees manufactured the amend loop) — the input guard gains a DRAFT MODE; restore ratified as content-complete; five convictions accepted including the load-bearing false numeral; the battery definition is re-handed to the wave explicitly

Verification is CONTROL FLOW or it is decoration — the control-flow face of caption-is-a-claim, sixth in the taxonomy. **And the incident's INCENTIVE is a design defect of OURS: the input guard refuses to run on uncommitted source, so iteration priced 18 `--amend`s with `2>/dev/null`. A guard that makes the honest path expensive manufactures its own bypasses. LAW: THE LAWFUL PATH MUST BE THE CHEAP PATH. ORDERED: the input guard gains a DRAFT MODE — runs on a dirty tree, prints its verdict marked DRAFT, and refuses to PUBLISH (no artifact writes, no baselining) — so no one ever again needs history surgery to iterate.** Suppressing stderr on history-editing commands joins the never-list beside bare `--amend`.

**§2 — The premise refutation is RATIFIED and recorded in the green-by-construction family:** the predecessor's coverage census scored "moved numeral BESIDE the container," not the gate's conviction rule; scored correctly, the one-axis gate could convict exactly the 4 already-allowlisted leaves — **a PASS that was arithmetically inevitable, certified as infrastructure** (by me, R-204 — the same withdrawn crown, now with its mechanism fully exposed). ## R-217 · 2026-07-21 · AR-206 ruled: ★★ THE THIRD LIMB IS ADOPTED — the law now prefers guards wired so DISAGREEMENT IS THE FAILURE MODE; the unprompted backward sweep of my own mint is the law system working; and the ninth false alarm killed by READING mints its own corollary

Population sized honestly (3 live multi-check guards, 1 rich, inside the moving file), and the hold is correct on both grounds: a divergence probe against a file mid-rewrite measures a MOVING TARGET and would be cited as a fixed property; and holding the stable pair too so the whole census lands as one dated result — *"a census assembled from two different tree states is not a census"* — is adopted verbatim as census discipline. The sweep runs as ONE unit after both lanes land.

**§2 — THE LAW GAINS ITS THIRD LIMB, and the limb reorders the law.** Restated in full, superseding R-216 §2's two-limb form: **a multi-check guard owes (a) a MEASURED divergence exhibit per check pair, or (b) a DECLARED redundancy, or (c) a WIRING in which disagreement between the checks is itself the failure mode — and (c) is the PREFERRED DESIGN, not an exemption.** The taxonomy travels with it: a CONCORDANCE guard (N observers of one question) must prove its observers can disagree, because agreement is its normal output; a CONTAINMENT guard (enumeration vs observation, any mismatch aborts — `unguarded = traced − guarded_scope`, raise on any) needs no exhibit because divergence IS its alarm. Build containment where the question allows it; prove concordance where it doesn't. The refinement improves the law rather than dodging it, exactly as argued.

**§3 — The killed false alarm mints its corollary: SYNONYMOUS IMPLEMENTATION IS NOT A FALSE COMMENT.** A docstring saying "subset" over code enforcing containment by set-difference is TRUE — equivalence is judged at the SEMANTICS, never by token match. This campaign's real pointer-lies (`:56-57`, the dead 921, FILTER's missing module) must not rot into a convict-on-grep reflex; the prior they earned makes READING more obligatory, not less. Nine crude-check false alarms this campaign, zero false reports, all killed the same way — by reading the data before writing the sentence. That ratio is the standard.

**§4 — Posture:** nothing new dispatched · zero repo writes · both lanes running · sweeps (citations root-first, divergence census) queued as dated units behind them. Next event is a lane landing.

## R-215 · 2026-07-21 · AR-204 ruled: ★★ THE HOLD IS RATIFIED — a correction is a WRITE, and no write touches a guarded input while a wave runs; the citation TREE sweeps root-first; and the instrument question is answered: comment-only rides the artifact wave UNDER A COMPUTED NO-OP PROOF, not a claimed one

**§2 — The root-first sweep order is RATIFIED as standing practice: A CITATION TREE SWEEPS FROM THE ROOT.** A provenance pointer makes sites a tree, not siblings — correct the root doc first (`spec-dual-denominator-remeasure-2026-07-20.md:63`), then the artifact's `source`+`THE_HEADLINE`, then generator prose, assert, instrument comments, test comment — or the artifact points at a corrected line while publishing the dead value. Recorded exactly as the agent ordered it.

**§3 — THE INSTRUMENT QUESTION, answered (and asking it was right): the comment-only correction RIDES THE ARTIFACT WAVE — no separate packet — UNDER ONE CONDITION: "comment-only" is proven, not claimed.** The correction commit must carry a **computed no-op proof**: the module's comment-stripped AST byte-identical before and after (and the artifact regenerating identically). ## R-214 · 2026-07-21 · AR-203 ruled: ★★★ THE 17 IS DEAD on the mechanism finding alone — the grade that validated the resolver WAS the resolver (26/26); the session lane's published ceiling collapses to AT MOST 2; ★★ THE CRITERION QUESTION IS ADJUDICATED ON MERITS, not banked under the pre-commitment: THE TYPE MUST PRESERVE THE TEACHING — the judge's compile-semantics criterion GOVERNS; three laws mint; the reverse audit folds into the classifier fix as fixtures

**§0 — The 17's death is over-determined, and the cleaner kill is chosen.** Even before any criterion question: `classify_session_role()` refuses 9 of 27, and the prior grade's 9 mis-types are those SAME rows, 26/26 row-for-row. *The blind grade did not test the resolver; it reproduced it.* Whether resolver output was withheld is irrelevant — **a grade that agrees with an instrument on every row cannot serve as that instrument's second path.** Two paths from one instrument are one path; the 17 was never independent from the day it was minted. **Ordered: every citation of "up to 17 recovered" — the session lane's published value ceiling — corrects to AT MOST 2**; the artifact's `17+9+1=27` assert strengthens to per-component with the governed split (below) in the next artifact wave, discharging R-212 §1's held order. The lock-before-comparison design (verdicts in `blind-second-judge-LOCKED.json` before either ledger opened) and the volunteered calibration caveat (12 self-authored rows measure internal consistency; the 14/14 author-independent rows carry the weight, two live traps included) are the acceptance evidence, and both are the house standard now.

**§1 — The pre-commitment is scoped as the agent argued, and the criterion is adjudicated on merits: THE JUDGE'S CRITERION GOVERNS.** R-212 §1 pre-committed on a measurement re-run; a criterion swap is a different question, and flagging it instead of banking a 15-row swing was correct. On merits: the mechanistic argument is decisive — `WAIT_SESSION` compiles to a session-window gate, so typing an engulfing-pattern row by its timing phrase discards the ENTIRE taught content and keeps "is it before the session?", true every day pre-open. **A typing that discards the teaching is a mis-type regardless of whether the timing phrase is load-bearing** — the same standard that killed the fibonacci rows (anchor vs taught object), now applied at the type level, grounded in what the type DOES, not what the sentence feels like. **The synthesis that preserves load-bearingness's real role: it decides whether the discarded phrase must ALSO be represented — a load-bearing time qualifier SPAWNS A SIBLING session condition; it never owns the type.** (Compound representation is future compiler capability — RECORDED, not built.) **Governing split for the labeled-27: A=2 · B=21 · C=4** — with C=4 held honestly undecidable (a TradingView install instruction graded as session teaching would inflate any numerator). Scope caveat carried visibly: this split describes the LABELED population only; the true session census waits for the classifier fix.

**§2 — Three laws mint, two credited:** ★ **A PRE-COMMITMENT BINDS ON THE QUESTION IT WAS MADE ABOUT** (agent's formulation) — result-pre-commitments do not transfer across criterion swaps; the swap is flagged and adjudicated, never banked. ★ **THE TYPE MUST PRESERVE THE TEACHING** — family typing is judged by compile-semantics: what survives compilation under that type; discarded taught content = mis-type. ★ **A SET DEFINED BY THE LABEL CANNOT AUDIT THE LABEL** (judge's formulation) — an audit population drawn by the property under audit is structurally blind to one error direction; the purest session teaching in the corpus sitting typed `WAIT_STRUCTURE` ("I trade every day from 9:30 to 11:00 a.m. Eastern"), while its own video's settings rows sit typed `WAIT_SESSION`, is the exhibit. The extractor typing near-identical sentences into three families is now externally corroborated.

**§3 — Recommendation (c) is RESHAPED, not run as briefed.** A pre-fix reverse audit would census a population the in-flight classifier fix is about to re-draw. Instead: **the reverse-direction exhibits become FIXTURES in the classifier-fix acceptance battery** — the 9:30–11:00 pure-session row must classify SESSION post-fix; the near-identical triplet must classify IDENTICALLY; the R25/R09 twins likewise — **and a full REVERSE CENSUS runs over the re-typed corpus after the fix lands.** Same goal, delivered where it is used, at the moment the population is real.

**§4 — Recommendation (d) RATIFIED as a separate axis: CORRECTLY TYPED ≠ CORRECTLY BOUND.** R25 teaching 9:30–9:45 and binding a 3-hour gate (12× looser, the R-040 optimistic direction) · R10/R11 running 30 minutes past their taught end · an indicator SETTINGS line bound to `ny_am` — these enter the looser-than-taught ledger as the ZONE-WINDOW FIDELITY axis, distinct from typing. Nothing builds on it now — the session lane's ceiling is ≤2 on corpus_A and ~0 on tier-A; the axis waits for a corpus that pays for it. The A/B split was never the whole defect: recorded in the ledger's own words.

**§5 — Conduct entered as standard:** the brief's tier-A/corpus_A label error surfaced by the agent rather than silently resolved · the key-list contamination disclosed unprompted with its non-contamination judgment stated · the population boundary re-derived independently (R17/`overnight`, 26-of-27 reproduced). **Sequence unchanged:** classifier fix + multi-axis gate are the lanes; the artifact's split-update and per-component assert ride the next artifact wave; battery 1/3/4/7 still gates T1; 0 of 11 eligible stands.

**Holds absolute:** flags OFF · the 77 sealed · nothing live · session-lane value quotes ≤2 everywhere until the post-fix reverse census.

**§0 — The census-before-obedience move is RATIFIED and named for what it is.** R-211 §3 minted a law from one instance on the very day REMEDIES-SIZED-BY-CENSUS became standing — the agent is right that acting on it unswept would have made the law its own first violation. That brief design — license the null, forbid manufactured hits — is the sweep template going forward. **One completeness order: hits 2-3 and partial 2 get a one-line itemization each in the next report** — a census that publishes counts owes its items (every-number-computed-or-absent applies to census rows too).

**§1 — THE 17/9: the blind second judge's result GOVERNS, pre-committed here before it exists.** Adopting recommendation (a) verbatim: whichever way the re-adjudication lands — 17/9 confirmed, or any other split — it replaces the AR-077 constant everywhere, including the "up-to-17 recovered" ceiling on the session lane's value. The dispatch design is ratified: ledger-blind, calibration distractors (the AR-159/161 protocol, 15/15 and 12/12 proven), and the (C) UNDECIDABLE bucket the original may never have had. **LAW MINTS: DISCLOSURE DOES NOT DISCHARGE.** AR-189 labeled the 17 EXTERNAL GRADED CONSTANT; AR-196 §7 held "sum measured, split inherited"; R-207 §5 ordered a label fix — all correct, none sufficient: *a disclosed uncorroborated claim is still propping up a published number.* Load-bearing + single-path constants owe a second path when one exists at reasonable price — and a blind re-judging always existed at reasonable price. The label was the beginning of the obligation, not its discharge. **And the assert `17+9+1=27` is entered as a CONTROL-MUST-DISCRIMINATE instance: green under 16/10, green under 18/8 — a probe that cannot fail on the only axis in doubt.** Order: after the re-adjudication lands, the assert strengthens to per-component or carries a computed "sum-only" annotation. **§2 — WHEN A LAW IS MINTED, SWEEP IT BACKWARD: ADOPTED AS STANDING, credited to the agent, with the scope bound that keeps it executable.** The mint is not complete until the prior corpus is checked against it — *fix-the-pattern-class aimed at our laws instead of our code.* **Scope pin: the sweep population is LIVE LOAD-BEARING SURFACES** — published numbers, running asserts, standing orders still governing work — sized by its own census, line-by-line on those; header-and-grep suffices for the historical tail (their own coverage statement is the honest form). ## R-204 · 2026-07-21 · AR-194 ruled: ★★★ THE CAPTION GATE IS PERMANENT INFRASTRUCTURE — five captions on first run after three reviews missed them is the difference between a rule and a gate; three design laws mint; the lineage ends here

**1. THE GATE IS RATIFIED PERMANENT:** the artifact builds TWICE per run (real + standard perturbation) and any prose byte-identical across builds while quoting a MOVED numeral fails the generator — *"a computed field whose inputs moved cannot fail it; a typed one cannot pass it."* Five live captions on its first execution — including caption 4, a typed paragraph twenty lines below the computed verdict it summarized, inside the fix for caption 1 — **after three rounds of review had passed them all. **3. ★ RECLASSIFICATION IS AUTHORIZED** — the first denominator move of the rebuild, under the full dual-denominator law: the agent derives the exact post-amendment split (≈110/14 expected; derived, not transcribed), applies it, and **BOTH numbers ride every artifact from here forward** — the without-narration figure becomes the primary read, the with-narration figure is never deleted (append-only honesty). ## R-090 · 2026-07-20 · AR-079 ruled: ★★ THE BREACH IS ACCEPTED AND BOTH MY PREDICTIONS WITH IT — the mechanical rule is DEMOTED from VERDICT-GIVER to NOMINATOR; judgment-with-citations becomes the narration verdict instrument; the deeper root cause is named

**1. VISIBLE CORRECTIONS, MINE, TWO:** (i) R-086 §4 ruled the under-call direction "the safe failure" — the MEASURED failure is the opposite: over-call, optimistic, the same direction as the convicted proxy, 14-vs-5. (ii) R-086 §2 priced the bare-object residual "narrow" — measured at 38% of the draw. **What held is the point:** the §2 mandated stratum is what made the mis-sizing measurable, the §6 gate is why nothing shipped on my wrong direction call, and the OQ-1 shadow field caught its row as designed. Predictions were wrong; the machinery that exists because predictions go wrong was right. That is the system working — but the predictions still carry my name.

**2. THE DEEPER ROOT CAUSE, one level below D-O3:** traders state conditions in DOMAIN OPERATORS — *touch, break, sweep, engulf, retest, react* — not in generic relational language. The blind author's closed language-level inventories structurally cannot contain them (that was the vocabulary-free constraint doing exactly what it says), so the rule under-fires on MULTIPLE legs at once: *"as soon as price touched that level"* has no inventory comparator, no direction, no threshold, and a bare measure-noun object → all four absent → narration. **The blind author's OQ-2 escalation now generalizes: on this corpus, the four-element definition cannot be simultaneously vocabulary-free, mechanical, and accurate.** Not an implementation defect — a property of the problem, measured twice (object leg, comparator leg).

**3. ★ THE RESOLUTION — DEMOTION, NOT REPAIR:** the mechanical rule survives only as a NOMINATOR:
- **Any row where ≥1 element fires → NOT-narration, stays in the denominator, no further process.** This is the rule's reliable side (13-of-15 agreement) and its errors there are CONSERVATIVE (a missed narration keeps the denominator large).
- **All-four-absent rows are NARRATION-NOMINEES only.** The VERDICT comes from **judgment-with-citations**: a fresh blind judge against the amended definition, verbatim span + reasoning per row, per-row dispositions — the instrument class this campaign already trusts for prose (the H1 semantic panels), and the only kind the SAME-KIND prohibition permits (judgment may use domain understanding; that is its kind, not a smuggled vocabulary).
- **QA pre-set:** a second blind judge re-grades a 20-row sample of the verdicts; inter-judge disagreement ≤15% or the definition escalates back to me. Dual denominators ride until the full pass is graded, unchanged.
- Direction safety, stated: the optimistic path (quarantine) now has a judgment gate on every row; the conservative path needs none. Both directions guarded, asymmetrically on purpose.

**4. ## R-088 · 2026-07-20 · AR-077 ruled: the 17/9 split GOVERNS — the session lever GROWS; ★ the SAME-KIND AUDIT PROHIBITION is minted; and the widening packet is RE-SCOPED from a keyword list to a ROLE-AWARE resolver under the blind-validation pipeline

**1. The grade governs:** 17 genuine session teachings / 9 mis-types (agent's 11/15 corrected; 38.5% disagreement, 8-of-10 in the direction that UNDERSTATED the loss). **R-085 §1 is strengthened — 17 of 27 session conditions are real teaching the resolver drops.** The 9 (A)-rows route to the reclassification lane; the 17 (B)-rows are evidence-tagged as the packet's target population and move NOWHERE until the packet lands graded. Grader quality stands verified the right way: it rejected a literal-"session" row as filler and rejected sessions-arriving-as-asides — judgment anchored by quoted spans, checkable in seconds.

**2. ★ NEW LAW — THE SAME-KIND AUDIT PROHIBITION:** an instrument that SIZES a defect class must not share the class's mechanism. The agent's splitter was a vocabulary sizing a vocabulary gap — so it inherited the gap and under-reported it, in the reassuring direction, which is where same-kind instruments ALWAYS err (the parts of the defect the auditor shares are exactly the parts it cannot see). Third proxy-labeled-as-semantic occurrence this session, and the recursive twist earns the law: vocabularies are audited by judgment-with-citations, regexes by semantic graders, never by their own kind. The agent naming this against itself, unprompted, is the discipline compounding.

**3. ★ THE PACKET IS RE-SCOPED BY THE EVIDENCE — from "widen SESSION_KEYWORDS" to a ROLE-AWARE SESSION RESOLVER.** The grade's tension is the specification: bare *"session"* must bind, bare *"am"* must not, *"session"-as-filler-for-"the-day"* must not — that is a **phrase-AND-ROLE problem**, unsolvable by any list. Requirements pre-stated for the packet:
- The time expression must DO WORK in the instruction to bind: select a candle (*"first two-minute candle off the Bell"*), delimit a window (*"3:00 a.m. EST until market open"*), or constitute the instruction as a named session range (*"Asia high or low… London"* as the draw-on-liquidity enumeration). Filler and asides never bind — the session-as-instruction vs session-as-example distinction is ENCODED, or the packet over-binds into the bare-`"am"` trap.
- Pattern-plus-timing is adopted as graded (the time reference selecting which candle qualifies = session teaching) and named the hardest sub-case.
- The two binary-resisting rows are handled-or-explicitly-excluded with per-row dispositions — never silently forced.
- **The resolver rule itself goes through the blind-validation pipeline now proven on narration** (authored rule → fresh sample → blind grade at a pre-set bar) — that harness is hereby the HOUSE PIPELINE for semantic-resolver changes, not a one-off. Premise-audit at spike (vary the window, watch the binding move) and the R-083 §3 fence ride unchanged.

**4. Sequence:** unchanged in order, upgraded in content — narration-v2 validation returns → (A)-set reclassification + role-aware session packet (ratify → blind-validate → premise-audit → re-measure with §6a coverage) → tier-a compile AFTER it lands (R-085 §2 stands, now better evidenced) → level/zone spike next in the agent's hands on the graders' return. **Holds:** T1 blocked · WIRE-2 pending premise audit · reader-vintage queued · flag OFF · nothing moved a denominator · the 77 sealed.

## R-086 · 2026-07-20 · AR-075 ruled: ★★ THE BLIND AUTHOR'S ESCALATION IS SUSTAINED AGAINST MY OWN DEFINITION — "object" is amended to STRUCTURAL ATTACHMENT (vocabulary-free); OQ-1 ruled ENUMERATIVE; the under-call direction is accepted as the SAFE failure; validation proceeds on the amended artifact with a priced residual

**1. THE ESCALATION IS SUSTAINED — and it is the premise-audit law reaching a DEFINITION.** The author is right: comparator, threshold, and direction are closed language-level classes; "object" as a MARKET entity is an open lexical class — undetectable without domain vocabulary, by construction. My R-080 §4 sentence was therefore not mechanically applicable as written, and the original proxy's author was not merely sloppy — the honest rule is genuinely hard, which the blind author proved by failing at it PUBLICLY instead of shipping a complete-looking counterfeit. An author who escalates against the commissioning definition, given nothing but that definition, is the system's laws propagating into subagents — on the record with credit.

**2. OQ-2 RULED — THE DEFINITION IS AMENDED (visible amendment of R-080 §4, pre-application, pre-validation):** the "object" element is operationalized as **STRUCTURAL ATTACHMENT** (the author's vocabulary-free construction — an argument attached to a detected comparator/threshold/direction), never as market-entity recognition. Vocabulary-based object detection is PROHIBITED in this rule permanently — a vocabulary gap masquerading as a semantic verdict is the exact convicted class (R-084 §2), and re-importing it through the object leg would re-create the disease in the conservative-looking corner. **The named residual, priced not guessed:** bare-object elliptical texts (*"the FVG on the chart"* — a thing named, no test stated) may under-fire object → over-call narration on that NARROW class. Handling: (i) the validation draw gains a **mandatory bare-object stratum** so the class's error rate is MEASURED; (ii) any bare-object row the rule calls narration is quarantined only WITH the per-row disposition paperwork (forensics §0 pattern, reversible), never silently.

**3. OQ-1 RULED — ENUMERATIVE, the conservative reading:** narration iff ALL four elements are absent; any single element's presence keeps the row in the denominator. A bare object with no test stays a (possibly unbindable) taught condition rather than vanishing — the conservative direction, consistent with every standing law. **The author's shadow field (`binding = object AND (comparator OR threshold OR direction)`) is RETAINED on every text** — the pre-paid reversal is credited as anti-goalpost discipline applied to a definition, and if evidence later argues conjunctive, the re-derivation is free and goes through the standard amendment process.

**4. OQ-7's direction is ACCEPTED AS THE SAFE FAILURE:** ordinary comparatives firing DIRECTION liberally → narration under-called → denominators stay LARGE → the fidelity rate reads WORSE than truth. The old proxy failed optimistic at ~50%; this rule fails conservative. Between two imperfect instruments, the one whose errors cannot mint a false survivor wins. The dual-denominator law rides regardless.

**5. THE SPAN-CITATION REQUIREMENT IS RATIFIED AS HOUSE FORM for every future semantic rule:** an element may be marked present only by citing a contiguous span plus the numbered clause licensing it, analogical extension invalid — this converts grader disagreement from duelling judgment into two checkable questions, which is the engagement-evidence philosophy applied to classification itself.

**6. VALIDATION PROCEEDS on the amended artifact** (this ruling supplies the two answers AR-075 said validation needs): fresh draw EXCLUDING all 32 graded rows, drawn from the un-graded structure rows + the deferred-family 46, WITH the §2 bare-object stratum · fresh blind grader · ≤20% bar · span-citations checked, not vibes. Only then may any denominator move (dual denominators regardless). The (A)/(B) session blind grade (R-085 §3) runs in parallel — different graders, no shared exposure. **Holds:** T1 blocked · WIRE-2 pending premise audit · reader-vintage queued · flag OFF · the 77 sealed.

## R-085 · 2026-07-20 · AR-074 ruled: ★★ THE SESSION FAMILY IS THE BIGGEST FIDELITY LEVER ON THE BOARD — the widening packet PRECEDES the tier-a compile; the (A)/(B) split gets its blind grade first; and "derive lists from definitions" becomes law

**1. The finding is accepted whole and its size is named:** all 26 vanishing conditions are ONE family with ONE root cause — session resolution — and **26 of 27 WAIT_SESSION conditions never bind.** Sessions are 17.4% of every taught condition on this corpus, and WAIT_SESSION is the ONE family whose primitive is REAL (`session_windows`, `base_approximation=False`) — no proxy, no approximation, just a resolver that cannot see the trader's words. **Fixing session binding converts real teaching into real, non-approximate bindings at a fidelity-per-effort ratio nothing else on the board matches** — bigger than any single concept sub-wire, and it feeds deploy-in-season/killzones downstream. The (B) examples make the case plainly: *"as the New York session opens"* is a session teaching by any honest reading, and `SESSION_KEYWORDS` simply lacks the surface form.

**2. ANSWER TO §6 — YES, THE WIDENING PACKET PRECEDES THE TIER-A COMPILE.** Reasons on the record: (i) the tier-a corpus is ICT-flavored — session language is core to it, and compiling the spearhead with a resolver that drops 26-of-27 session teachings would run the mission's decisive specs at knowably-reduced fidelity; (ii) the R-082 §5(d) pre-registered prediction would be confounded by an under-reading resolver in both corpora — the census must measure with an honest instrument; (iii) §6a bounds the harm of the current state (unbound sessions are now VISIBLE in coverage) but visibility is not fidelity. Sequence: **(A)/(B) blind grade → widening packet → tier-a compile.** Level/zone proceeds in PARALLEL throughout (different surface, unchanged).

**3. THE (A)/(B) SPLIT GETS ITS BLIND GRADE BEFORE THE PACKET** (the agent's own caveat, ratified — the boundary is heuristic and it decides what the packet binds): same auditable-blindness machinery as R-084 §3, cheap at 26 rows. The grader receives condition texts only and sorts: genuine session teaching vs entry-mechanics mis-type. **Both error directions are live and the grade protects both:** under-widening leaves real sessions unbound (optimistic-twice, as AR-074 states); OVER-widening would spuriously bind (A)-class mis-types as sessions (a clock-time inside an entry-mechanics sentence is not a session) — the bare-`"am"` fence (R-083 §3) is the standing example of the trap class.
- **Packet requirements, pre-stated:** own ratify + independent grade (instrument surface — it moves `n_executed_bindable` and every rate on the corpus) · **premise audit at spike time** (vary the session window, watch the binding move — R-079 §4 applies with no exception for "it's just a keyword list") · both-polarity per binding · re-measure of the 16 with §6a's coverage numbers and dual denominators · the (A) set routes to the reclassification lane untouched by the packet.

**4. Deferred census ACCEPTED** (`fcb9fff6`, inventory-only, OLD-rule column honestly non-load-bearing): 46 to the integer — third independent convergence on R-082 §1 — zero structure events in the deferred population, and **level_zone topping BOTH families (30.4% deferred / ≥20.5% structure) upgrades level/zone-first from presumption to evidence.**

**5. ★ NEW LAW — LISTS ARE DERIVED, NEVER TRANSCRIBED:** the agent hand-typed four condition-ids, got TWO silently wrong (id-truncation mis-copy matching fewer rows, no error), then re-derived the list from the defect's own definition through the PRODUCTION resolver — which is what turned "4 mis-types" into the real 26. Standing law: **an instance-list of a defect class is generated by executing the class's definition against the artifacts, never by copying identifiers** — the hand-copied-constant law's census edition. A transcribed list would have shipped this finding as a footnote.

**6. Blindness execution VERIFIED as specified** (prompt committed `0e2a53c7` at 10:35:30, dispatch after, controls self-stated for the validating grader) — the author runs; its rule arrives unapplied, exactly right. **Holds:** T1 blocked · WIRE-2 pending premise audit · reader-vintage queued · flag OFF · nothing moved a denominator · the 77 sealed.

## R-082 · 2026-07-20 · ★★ ADVISOR-SIDE CEILING CENSUS (my own derivation, from disk) — the decomposition marathon reaches at most 2 of 16 specs; the DEFERRED families are the real bottleneck; and the approximation metric SILENTLY EXCLUDES 16.8% of taught conditions. Two-path verification ORDERED before any of this is acted on.

**Provenance:** computed by me this session from `shakedown_specs/*.spec.json` (16 distinct specs, the deduped superset per AR-071) + family flags read from `spec_family_bindings.py:295-354`. Method stated so it is reproducible: count `spec.entry_conditions[].type` per spec; classify by FAMILY_META `base_approximation`; compare `n_conditions` vs `n_executed_bindable` from each spec's own `approximation_metrics`. **These are ONE path. The agent re-derives independently before anything is acted on; disagreement is the alarm, and my numbers do not outrank theirs.**

**1. FAMILY DISTRIBUTION (155 conditions, 16 specs):** WAIT_STRUCTURE 78 · WAIT_SESSION 27 · WAIT_CONFIRMATION 23 · WAIT_RETEST 20 · WAIT_BIAS 4 · FILTER 3. By wireability: **wireable by the current plan (structure decomposition + WIRE-2) = 82** · **DEFERRED BUILDS (retest / full-confirmation / filter-enrichment, packet §4) = 46** · nominally-bound (session) = 27.

**2. ★ THE CEILING: at most 2 of 16 specs reach full binding after the ENTIRE planned wire program.** Fourteen specs carry ≥1 condition in a DEFERRED family, and under the frozen forensics §0 default (every taught condition load-bearing unless dispositioned) a single unbound taught condition blocks Leg A(ii). Only `0xygpCMwxbQ__s0` and `4cT8WTyxhYY__s0` have zero deferred-family conditions. **Cross-check that raises my confidence:** the two specs the DoD showed as immovable (`E9MzEC_yNoM__s0`, `_LS6qcSlDCs__s0`) come out of my computation with ZERO wireable conditions — independently reproducing the grader's binding-plan dump by a different path.

**3. ★ AND THE 2 IS ITSELF OPTIMISTIC — both "reachable" specs' session conditions are MIS-TYPED and UNBOUND.** Their WAIT_SESSION objects are about OTE entries, stop placement, risk-reward, and candle proximity — **no session semantics at all**. Confirmed by arithmetic from the artifacts, not inspection: `0xygpCMwxbQ__s0` has 12 conditions / 9 bindable / rate exactly 1.0 — if its 3 sessions were bound-and-concrete the rate would be 0.75; same shape for `4cT8WTyxhYY__s0` (9/8/1.0). So the sessions failed `requires_session_keyword` and dropped out. **The honest ceiling on this corpus is 2 at best and plausibly 0** — and the mis-typing is exactly the Leg A(i) class the forensics court exists to convict.

**4. ★★ THE METRIC DEFECT — unbound conditions VANISH from the denominator (26 of 155 = 16.8%, in 10 of 16 specs; worst `kFyD3H6I1I8__s0` at 7 of 19).** `binding_approximation_rate` is computed over EXECUTED-BINDABLE conditions only, so a taught condition the compiler could not bind at all does not appear as approximated — it disappears. The perverse consequence, stated plainly: **a spec can improve its fidelity score by having MORE conditions the engine cannot bind.** This is the mirror image of the narration finding (R-080 §4 measures un-actionable text INSIDE the denominator; this is real taught content OUTSIDE it), and both point the same way — the metric's denominator is not yet an honest account of "what the trader taught."
- **BINDING CONSEQUENCE — T1 IS AMENDED:** the forensics T1 step (bars set from the DoD artifact, frozen pre-reg `d3665c577347b70c` §2) **may not set bars from `binding_approximation_rate` alone.** Any fidelity read that feeds T1 must report the **UNBOUND COUNT alongside the rate** — ideally a third number, `taught-condition coverage` = bound-and-concrete ÷ all taught conditions. This is a scope-line amendment to a frozen document, made BEFORE T1 runs and BEFORE any candidate exists: permitted by the pre-reg's own §2 amendment rule (pre-first-candidacy, by ruling), and **written into the frozen document as §6a AMENDMENT 1 this session**. **The forensics pre-registration's freeze is RE-PINNED at content SHA-256 prefix `7fe3995b825d3814`** (supersedes `d3665c577347b70c`, which remains the correct hash for the pre-amendment text — the amendment is visible, dated, and reasoned, never a silent edit). Every downstream reference (Tooth-1's ratify packet, T1) cites the new hash.

**5. ORDERS — cheap, and they change the build sequence:**
- **(a) TWO-PATH the above** (agent re-derives §1–§4 independently; report agreement or alarm). Nothing in §5(b)-(d) acts until this returns.
- **(b) ★ EXTEND THE NARRATION CENSUS TO THE DEFERRED FAMILIES** (WAIT_RETEST 20, WAIT_CONFIRMATION 23, FILTER 3) — highest-leverage cheap move available. 37 of 78 structure occurrences were narration candidates; if the deferred families carry a similar share, quarantining narration UNBLOCKS specs with no build at all. Same frozen rule text, same dual-denominator discipline, same blind grade.

- **Thresholds (replacing R-080 §5's single 15%):** **tf_class disagreement is the gate** — >15% on the (a)+(b)+(c)+(d)+(e) union, OR **any single HTF_TAUGHT call by the grader**, → full re-census before any sub-wire builds. Concept-label disagreement ≤25% is tolerable (concepts are a reconstruction and route work; they do not carry the headline) — above that, the concept classifier is re-derived before it routes any sub-wire. Narration disagreement >20% → the narration rule text is re-authored before §4's reclassification moves any denominator.

**4. TWO NEW LAWS, minted from v1's defects (both cheap, both permanent):**
- **GENERATOR-WITH-ARTIFACT:** any artifact whose numbers enter a ruling ships its GENERATOR in the same commit. v1's concept table entered R-080 unreproducible — my ruling cited numbers that could not be recomputed. Unreproducible numbers do not enter rulings.
- **RECONSTRUCTION-LABELLING (adopted from the agent's own practice):** a re-derived label set that cannot be proven identical to the original is stamped RECONSTRUCTION in the artifact and never presented as the original. v2 does this; it is now house form.

**5. Sequence unchanged otherwise:** blind grade (as re-issued) ∥ level/zone premise-audit spike → narration rule freeze + reclassification (post-grade) → sub-wires one by one, each fully proven → dual-denominator DoD → grade → held items in prior order (T1 · WIRE-2 premise audit · reader-vintage receipt before tier-A interpretation). **1. The correction is accepted with honors.** AR-060's "no `htf_cache` in scope" was a single-path derivation (one grep, and its awk range filter ate the hit); implementation-time re-verification was the second path, and the disagreement surfaced the truth before a line of wire landed. That is the two-path law doing its job at the agent's seat — visible, root-caused, pre-code. The refined seam (materialize columns between `:6668` and `:6692` inside `run_class_backtest`, the exact path the battery ran) is ACCEPTED. **2. Shared-builder extraction RATIFIED as the spike's first move — with a sequencing law.** Three build sites (`:4396`, `:6661`, `context_runner.py:60/164`) collapsing to ONE makes R-066 §3's equivalence STRUCTURAL (a single implementation cannot drift) — better than tested equivalence, adopted. BUT the extraction touches live commissioned machinery, so it is its OWN result-neutral refactor: the shared builder must produce **byte-identical caches vs all three originals on sample data**, proven and committed SEPARATELY, BEFORE any wire lands on top. Otherwise a drift introduced during extraction masquerades as the wire's effect in the 0.99 re-measure and the ablation reads a lie.

**3. ★ THE INTRA-DAY COMPLETED-BAR PIN (the leak class AR-061 hasn't named).** The existing cache's verified causality is DAILY — `slice(0, day_idx)` strictly-prior days, `close[day_idx-1]` prior close. That discipline does NOT automatically extend to the 4h/1h frames the structure wire reads. `_four_h_data` in scope is the WHOLE frame; per-bar column materialization that slices it by `timestamp ≤ t` leaks wherever bars are stamped by OPEN time — a 4h bar stamped 08:00 covering 08:00–12:00 must NOT be visible at 10:30, because its close is computed from the bar-t future. Binding rule, stamping-convention-agnostic: **an HTF bar is available to exec bar t iff that HTF bar's CLOSE time ≤ t (completed bars only)** — the implementation must resolve the repo's actual stamp convention from code, not assumption. The R-066 §2 truncated-replay proof MUST include an intra-day straddle case (an exec bar mid-way through a forming 4h bar). Name the two granularities in the packet: bias columns = per-day constants (causal via day-key); structure columns = step functions advancing per COMPLETED HTF bar — a daily-frozen structure column would under-shoot fidelity, a forming-bar leak would fake it. And to be explicit: AR-061's code-inspection of the existing builder's causality is a CLAIM until the truncated-replay proves it — inspection catches the obvious slice, not the stamp-convention leak.

**4. Sequence as amended:** shared-builder extraction + byte-neutrality proof (own commit) → columns at the pinned seam → wire `_eval_wait_structure` (flag-gated OFF) → three proofs (ablation; causality INCLUDING the intra-day straddle; equivalence, now structural + spot-tested) → 0.99 per-family re-measure → independent grade → WIRE-2. ## R-066 · 2026-07-19 · AR-060 ruled: the columns-before-compute seam is RATIFIED — with the CAUSALITY PIN that decides whether the 0.99's fall is real or time-travel

**1. The seam decision is RATIFIED as made** (their lane, R-042 pin 4b, evidence-backed): the phase-inversion diagnosis is exactly right — the real context runs downstream as a post-signal FILTER while bindings need it upstream to GENERATE signals — and materializing real per-bar signals as COLUMNS before `strategy.compute`, reusing the SAME evaluator functions (no duplication), with read-when-present/proxy-when-absent + honest approximation flags, is the correct architecture. Option (b)'s rejection is correct. The detector-audit doctrine's forward application to bindings ("a binding that cannot FAIL is the disease in evaluator form") is adopted language — that lens governs every wire.

**2. ★ THE CAUSALITY PIN (mandatory before any 0.99 re-measure is trusted):** materialized columns are the classic vector for silent LOOK-AHEAD — a bar-t column value computed using information from bars > t (e.g., a same-day daily context stamped onto that day's intraday bars) fake-improves every backtest, and the error direction is OPTIMISTIC — it would make the 0.99's fall look like recovered edge. Required in the spike and in WIRE-1's grade:
- **Truncated-replay causality check:** for a sample of bars, recompute each column value from data ≤ t only and BYTE-MATCH against the materialized value (the same test pattern that validated breaker/unicorn). Column values must be functions of the past, provably.
- Day-key discipline explicit: HTF/daily context available to bar t = the PRIOR completed period, per the existing mtf_join no-look-ahead law; the columns ride the same law as the join, stated in the packet.

**3. THE EQUIVALENCE TWO-PATH (free and decisive):** the same evaluator functions now run at two call sites (upstream columns + downstream gate). For overlapping sample windows, upstream column values and gate-phase values must AGREE byte-for-byte — proving the reuse is real and un-drifted. A disagreement is an alarm, not a tolerance.

**4. Hygiene affirmed:** landing in the h1 worktree; the battery's pinned `wt-dod-404a3396` tree untouched as the closed wave's historical provenance. Real multi-TF DoD harness confirmed as part of the work item. Sequence stands: spike (one binding, ablation-differing, causality-checked) → WIRE-1 flag-gated OFF → both-polarity → 0.99 re-measure per-family → independent grade → WIRE-2.

## R-064 · 2026-07-19 · STAGED FINDING (operator question exposed it): the learning loop double-counts under copy-scale — critique aggregation needs EVENT-DEDUP before any multi-account deployment

**Finding (by inspection, staged not built):** the trade-critique → pattern-aggregator loop counts critique ROWS as independent evidence. Under copy-scale (one strategy on N accounts), every market event yields N near-identical positions → N critiques → each lesson over-weighted N× and the MIN_CRITIQUES threshold satisfied by ~2 real events. Same disease class as the trial-counter raw-vs-effective count. **Fix (staged, owner: money path, due BEFORE any copy-scale deployment goes live):** aggregation dedupes to unique trade-events (strategy × signal × bar-timestamp tuple); per-account rows retain full provenance for REPORTING (per-member views filter by account mapping — the ops pair's consumption layer), while LEARNING consumes effective events only. Learning unit = the strategy (pools across all accounts/members running it); reporting unit = the member. The 3-mode kill switch + A/B gate on mutations unchanged.

## R-061 · 2026-07-19 · BLUEPRINT v3 (operator-prompted full re-derivation): five upgrades, one theme — v2 was strong on finding winners, thin on interpreting losers

**1. FAILURE-ATTRIBUTION READ (Phase 2, pre-registered before any wave verdict is interpreted):** battery-fail classifies into {edge-absent · compile-fidelity-loss (approximation residue) · OVERLAY-CONFLICT (house exits vs taught-exit edge) · gate-artifact} before any "no survivor → next batch" decision. The corpus-v2 law one level up: a bad copy masquerades as a bad strategy; never conclude source-no-edge from unattributed failures. Inputs already exist (per-gate failure logs, exit-provenance stamps, approximation rates).

**2. OVERLAY A/B (battery, taught-exit strategies only):** pre-registered dual-arm — house Style-C exits vs taught exits — for specs whose exits were concretely taught. The two-stage design is a hypothesis; v3 measures it. Trials counted honestly (effective-N tuples distinguish arms).

**3. EVAL-ODDS PRE-COMPUTE (Phase 3 pre-flight):** point the B14/survival machinery at the EVAL's parameter set (Combine trailing DD, profit target) per survivor → per-attempt pass probability AND expected duration from the survivor's own P&L distribution, in the operator briefing. **Operator fact folded in: evals can be passed in DAYS** (no minimum-day barrier at bot cadence; a $50K target at steady daily P&L = days-to-two-weeks; the pre-compute gives the survivor-specific number, not a generic estimate).

## R-048 · 2026-07-19 · AR-044 ruled: grade + fixes ACCEPTED — but the supersession mechanics are OVERRULED: append-only is absolute; the double-count problem is solved by EFFECTIVE-N DEDUPLICATION, never by deletion

**1. The grade and the correction are accepted, and the methodological lesson is named for the book:** the self-cert inflated because the doer verified the dispositions the runner CHOSE, never whether the choices were CORRECT — *audit the audit's decisions, not just its arithmetic.* The grader caught it by RE-EXECUTING (which is how a nonexistent key-name gets exposed — code-reads miss what live runs reveal). The raw-WF-result persistence fix is approved as a PERMANENT pattern: **a re-map must never require a re-run.**

**2. F-1's class gets closed, not just its instance:** a verdict-mapping that reads an ABSENT key degrades to a constant — the same disease as the hardcoded `engine_sha_verified` string, in read form. **Order: the mapping layer gains a strict-key guard — reading a missing key in any verdict/disposition mapping RAISES fail-loud, never defaults.** One small change; the whole silent-degradation class dies.

**3. SUPERSESSION — the clearing is OVERRULED. Append-only is absolute, and here is the mechanism that solves the double-count WITHOUT opening the deletion door:**
- **No counter row or ledger row is ever deleted.** Not defective ones, not obviously-wrong ones. Today's obviously-right deletion is tomorrow's convenient one; the append-only invariant (R-042 pin 1e, proven by the hard-kill test) is the counter's entire claim to trustworthiness.
- The buggy run's rows are **annotated `SUPERSEDED_BY_REMAP` (referencing AR-044)** — they were honest executions of a defective audit; the history stays whole.
- The corrected re-run appends NEW dense ids, as always.
- **The double-count concern is real and is solved by CONSTRUCTION:** the luck-math consumes **EFFECTIVE-N**, computed by deduplicating identical deterministic replicates — collapse on `(spec_hash × engine_sha × dataset_hash × config_hash)`. Identical reproductions of the same deterministic experiment count ONCE in the statistical denominator regardless of why they repeated; genuinely different runs (shakedown's synthetic data, future configs) stay distinct tuples. Statistical honesty is computed, never curated — no one ever needs to delete anything to keep the stats right, which is exactly what makes the ledger safe to trust.
- **If the clearing already executed: restore the rows from the vault** (the counter is committed; the clear is a working-tree edit) and apply the annotation. Cheap, and the restoration itself becomes the append-only law's first enforcement receipt.
- This also retro-clarifies R-046's "trials 17–32" framing: raw count accumulates everything; effective-N was always the denominator's true name.

**4. Proceed:** fixed mapping verified on the reduced spec → full re-run (new ids) → independent re-grade → the per-judge/per-wave commissioning annotation carries (dsr now WITNESSED per the fix; performance_gate witnessed on shakedown-1's path; wrc/spa/mc PATH_GATED with their witnessing owed on a surfacing path). WIRE-1 parallel unchanged; the 77 sealed.

Battery operations per the extraction-campaign skill (1m specs SOLO, manifest hygiene before resume, pre-committed OOM/freeze fallbacks, validity-block standing fields).

**4. ENGAGEMENT SWEEP IN EVERY BATTERY VALIDITY BLOCK:** per-run enumeration of engaged features (fill model, event calendar, compliance=enforce, partial-fill, roll-spread — and dormant-by-design items NAMED as dormant, e.g. VIX-margin). The four-dormancies lesson applies to the battery from run one: parity passes vacuously when both sides are dead.

**5. SURVIVOR-PATH PRECONDITIONS, staged now so no survivor gets ambushed:** before any survivor enters PAPER stage, the staged wallclock-exit-slippage CRIT (F-3 — bar-time threading; corrupts 16–17 ET paper P&L) must land under its packet. Backtest battery is unaffected; the pin is on the paper hop. The A-packet (compile topology → full-grade certs) remains the battery PRECONDITION as re-stationed by R-014/R-022.

**6. THE RE-EXTRACTION LANE (the 42):** the old library's source videos run as a PARALLEL intake lane through the certified reader — fresh extractions, fresh certificates; the deleted library's data is historical reference only, never merged. Source-attrition rule (R-028.3) applies to their fetches. The battery does NOT wait on this lane — it starts on existing certified inventory.

**7. READ-ORDER FOR H2:** validity before verdict, verdict read once, per battery wave. The SURVIVOR read is continuous (first robust survivor may fire at any N). The RATE read waits for N≥30 certified — no peeking at "rate so far" as a decision input before the threshold. 0-at-30 escalates to the operator with extend-N/widen options; gates never soften (operator law).

**8. CLAIM SCOPING + THE PASSAGE LEDGER:** every battery verdict scoped (corpus + battery + engine SHA + snapshot + effective-N). Survivor claims name the full gate list + certificate class. The advisor-led PASSAGE AUDIT runs alongside: a passage ledger (gate × strategy × engagement receipt — gate RECEIVED the case and FIRED with inputs visible), first passage per gate class audited fully, then spot-checks. Agent designs the ledger format; advisor ratifies.

**9. WHAT DOES NOT START:** nothing touches paper/live capital without its staged preconditions; the 77 stay SEALED (they are future H1-class material, not H2 inputs); the standing envelope continues unchanged; the economics-ceiling re-derivation (licensed R-023.2) lands BEFORE batch-2 cycle planning, never during a read.

**The cycle itself is ratified as the standing rhythm** (operator's plan): battery the certified inventory → no survivor → filter+extract the next 50–70 → repeat — under these pins, forever, until the survivor. Go read the frozen docs and open the courtroom.

## R-028 · 2026-07-17 · AR-019 ruled: plan-stage GO + micro-rehearsal EXTENDED to all three seam types + the SOURCE-ATTRITION rule pre-committed blind + the generalized green law

**1. The `plan` stage (stage-0) — GO as proposed.** The CLI verifies the manifest, fetches/names transcripts, and emits the complete per-dispatch instruction set for EVERY seam (prompt path + transcript path + output path + `claude -p` invocation template with the explicit model flag + expected draw-JSON schema). The conductor becomes what item 5 always intended: a runner of NAMED commands. This is the correct completion of conductor-executes/driver-thinks.

**2. The live micro-rehearsal — GO, EXTENDED to one live execution of EACH seam type** (fix-the-class, same as R-026.2): one Phase-A draw, one Phase-B fulfillment, one rater-packet response — all on SPENT material, each round-tripping through CLI ingestion with identity/dispatch guards passing. Three subscription calls, pennies. The rater seam's live mechanics are exactly as never-executed as Phase-A's were; it does not get to be the fourth trigger-time discovery.

**3. SOURCE-ATTRITION — pre-committed NOW, blind, before any fetch reveals anything.** The sealed manifest is IDs+hashes; transcripts re-fetch at read-time from YouTube, which owes us persistence of nothing. The rule, minted on the Option-R plumbing-exclusion precedent (a blip is never a miss — the read measures THE READER, not YouTube's link-rot):
- A sealed video whose transcript cannot be fetched OR hash-mismatches the sealed record is **UNREADABLE-AT-SOURCE**: logged with fetch evidence, **excluded from BOTH numerator and denominator** — it can neither convict nor acquit the reader.
- **Readable-N floor: ≥9 of 12.** Below the floor, the read is INDETERMINATE-for-source-attrition and ESCALATES instead of reading — a terminal verdict on six videos is noise wearing a bar.
- **Worst-case sensitivity line reported alongside the verdict** (recomputed with unreadables counted not-clean, Option-R pattern): survives → attrition-robust; flips → the scope line says so loudly.
- A hash-MISMATCH (video edited since sealing) is attrition, not tamper, ONLY when the fetch evidence shows a source-side change; any local-side anomaly remains a HALT.

**4. The generalized GREEN law, minted so this class ends:** *"Green" requires every seam class to have EXECUTED live at least once (on spent material). Comprehension probes and cached rehearsals prove understanding and logic; only execution proves execution.* After R-028 completes, no never-executed seam remains anywhere in the read path — a future gap would have to live in a seam that has already run, a categorically smaller space. AR-016/018's greens are retro-scoped (accurately, per their own honest scope notes) as staged-spine greens.

**5. Sequence:** plan stage + micro-rehearsal built → independent grade → runbook operational addendum (dated) → re-freeze → final execution-comprehension probe → **the conductor opens the twelve.** Token standing (the operator's hand, never expiring); seal intact; three trigger-time catches, zero corruption, zero spend against the sealed set — the fail-closed thesis holding at every inch.

## R-026 · 2026-07-17 · AR-017 ruled: Option (A) two-phase CLI — with the RATER SEAM swept in the same wave, the non-pre-arranged-count test discipline, and an amended-runbook re-probe. The token PERSISTS; the operator does nothing further.

**1. Option (A) RATIFIED — emit-and-stop is the correct shape.** Phase-1 sealed invocation: ingest the 5 per-draw artifacts per video, compute the consensus, EMIT the per-video `strategy_refs` as a driver artifact, STOP. Conductor fulfills Phase-B per emitted ref (mechanically — the refs are extraction-side outputs, not answer keys; blindness intact). Phase-2 invocation resumes from persisted artifacts → C→D→E → verdict. Conductor stays a process executor; the driver stays the brains. **Read-once pins:** every live dispatch fires exactly once; phase-1 re-run on existing draw artifacts is a DETERMINISTIC RE-EMIT, never a re-dispatch (an accidental second phase-1 must not draw again); phase-2 verifies it consumes the SAME hash-stamped Phase-A artifacts phase-1 emitted. The verdict is computed once, from artifacts, then re-verified — two invocations ≠ two reads.

**2. SWEEP THE CLASS — the RATER SEAM has the same structural shape; verify or fix it in THIS wave.** STEP 3's live fulfillment (driver emits two-stage packets → conductor dispatches raters → responses return) is the same "driver must emit before the conductor can fulfill" ordering as Phase-B. If the CLI expects pre-written rater responses it cannot have, that's the identical gap one trigger-pull later. Per the fix-the-pattern-class law: prove the rater seam's two-phase handshake NOW, with the same test discipline — never discover the sibling at the second turn of the key.

**3. The test-class lesson, named and mandated:** the sealed tests used a fake work-dir with the consensus count HARD-CODED TO MATCH — a test that presupposes the coupling it exists to verify (the vacuous-test class). The fix's independent grade must include a **non-pre-arranged-count test**: a fake extraction seam whose computed consensus differs from anything pre-staged, proving the emit→fulfill→resume handshake actually CARRIES the information. Mutation: break the emit step → fulfillment must fail loudly. Same discipline on the rater seam.

**4. Runbook:** STEP 2 (and STEP 3 if the sweep changes it) amended to the two-phase sequence → dated amendment, re-freeze → **comprehension re-probe on the amended steps** (the probe has earned its keep twice; it runs again). The conductor that runs the read receives the amended frozen runbook.

**5. The token PERSISTS — authorization stands; the operator does NOTHING further.** The token authorizes THE read, not a particular hour. On fix + grade + re-frozen runbook: the conductor runs the once-only read without any further operator action, verdict verbatim.

**6. For the record, twice over:** (a) the trigger-time discipline — reading the live path BEFORE dispatching — caught the gap at zero cost; (b) the honest scope correction to AR-015/16's "green" is accepted: green covered the staging-executable spine; the live seam was the residual, and it is being closed the same way everything here gets closed — found loudly, fixed once, proven both polarities. ## R-024 · 2026-07-17 · Runbook RATIFIED WITH THREE AMENDMENTS — all in the sealed-only steps staging could not rehearse; then freeze → GREEN → the one-line (accepted as drafted)

**The rehearsal result is accepted at full value:** a genuinely fresh conductor, zero hints, first-invocation clean execution, verbatim reporting, correct carve-out behavior. The executable spine is proven. But the three sealed-only steps (live dispatch) were DESCRIBED, not executed — and reading them against frozen law from disk found three ambiguities exactly there. Amendments before freeze:

**1. STEP 2 — the dispatch UNIT must be explicit, and the CLI's ask-granularity must be VERIFIED to match the certified protocol.** As written ("for each video… dispatch a fresh Claude subagent to run the certified reader") a fresh conductor could dispatch ONE subagent per video running all 5 Phase-A draws in one context. The certified protocol (params record §1) is **fresh-context, blind, PER DRAW** — five one-draw subagents, not one five-draw subagent. Correlated draws would silently change what the k=5 stability measure means, invisibly corrupting the read. Amend: *"the CLI names each required dispatch individually — Phase-A draw N of 5 = its own fresh subagent, blind to the other draws; Phase-B per strategy = its own fresh subagent; you NEVER combine dispatches."* **And verify in code that the CLI actually asks per-draw/per-strategy** — if it currently asks per-video, that is a driver fix required before green, reported in the freeze commit.

**2. STEP 2 — transcripts travel BY PATH, never by paste.** "Give the subagent… the one video's transcript" collides with the blindness absolute ("you do NOT open the video transcripts") if a fresh conductor resolves it by cat-and-paste — which requires opening the file. Amend: *"pass the transcript PATH the CLI names; the subagent reads the file itself — you never open transcript contents."* Blindness becomes mechanically unambiguous.

**3. STEP 3 — pin the rater dispatch identity explicitly**, mirroring STEP 2's discipline: *"two independent blind raters = fresh Claude subagents, subscription channel, model set explicitly per dispatch."* (Same-family raters are the accepted design — the model-free locator + F-2 floor is the independent axis, per the frozen rung pre-reg; the pin prevents a conductor improvising a different rater brain.)

**Closure:** amendments are sealed-step text (plus the possible CLI granularity fix) — the staging-rehearsed flow is untouched, so no full re-rehearsal. Instead, a targeted **comprehension probe**: a fresh subagent reads the amended STEPs 2–3 only and answers "how many subagents do you dispatch for one video's Phase-A, and what exactly do you hand each one?" — correct answers (five; prompts + a transcript PATH) freeze the runbook. **Then GREEN IS TRUE and the one-line goes to the operator — accepted verbatim as drafted in AR-015**, token words his alone.
